PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot1, crate1 is at depot1, crate2 is at depot0, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at depot1, truck1 is at depot1, truck2 is at distributor0, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate1 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet2 is clear, pallet3 is clear, crate0 is on pallet1, crate1 is on crate0 and crate2 is on pallet0.
My goal is to have that crate1 is on pallet1 and crate2 is on pallet3.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot1, crate1 is at depot1, crate2 is at depot0, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at depot1, truck1 is at depot1, truck2 is at distributor0, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate1 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet2 is clear, pallet3 is clear, crate0 is on pallet1, crate1 is on crate0 and crate2 is on pallet0.
My goal is to have that crate1 is on pallet1 and crate2 is on pallet3.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain depots)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types
    place surface hoist truck - object
    depot distributor - place
    pallet crate - surface
  )

  (:predicates
    (at ?obj - object ?p - place)
    (on ?c - crate ?s - surface)
    (in ?c - crate ?t - truck)
    (lifting ?h - hoist ?c - crate)
    (available ?h - hoist)
    (clear ?s - surface)
  )

  (:action drive
    :parameters (?t - truck ?from - place ?to - place)
    :precondition (at ?t ?from)
    :effect (and
      (not (at ?t ?from))
      (at ?t ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action lift
    :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?s - surface ?p - place)
    :precondition (and
      (at ?h ?p)
      (at ?c ?p)
      (available ?h)
      (clear ?c)
      (on ?c ?s)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at ?c ?p))
      (lifting ?h ?c)
      (not (available ?h))
      (clear ?s)
      (not (on ?c ?s))
      (not (clear ?c))
    )
  )

  (:action drop
    :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?s - surface ?p - place)
    :precondition (and
      (at ?h ?p)
      (at ?s ?p)
      (clear ?s)
      (lifting ?h ?c)
    )
    :effect (and
      (available ?h)
      (not (lifting ?h ?c))
      (at ?c ?p)
      (not (clear ?s))
      (clear ?c)
      (on ?c ?s)
    )
  )

  (:action load
    :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?t - truck ?p - place)
    :precondition (and
      (at ?h ?p)
      (at ?t ?p)
      (lifting ?h ?c)
    )
    :effect (and
      (available ?h)
      (not (lifting ?h ?c))
      (in ?c ?t)
    )
  )

  (:action unload
    :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?t - truck ?p - place)
    :precondition (and
      (at ?h ?p)
      (at ?t ?p)
      (available ?h)
      (in ?c ?t)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (in ?c ?t))
      (not (available ?h))
      (lifting ?h ?c)
    )
  )
)
</domain>

<problem>
(define (problem depots_problem)
  (:domain depots)
  (:objects
    depot0 depot1 depot2 - depot
    distributor0 - distributor
    truck0 truck1 truck2 - truck
    hoist0 hoist1 hoist2 hoist3 - hoist
    pallet0 pallet1 pallet2 pallet3 - pallet
    crate0 crate1 crate2 - crate
  )

  (:init
    ;; Locations of stationary objects
    (at hoist0 depot0)
    (at hoist1 depot1)
    (at hoist2 depot2)
    (at hoist3 distributor0)
    (at pallet0 depot0)
    (at pallet1 depot1)
    (at pallet2 depot2)
    (at pallet3 distributor0)

    ;; Locations of mobile objects
    (at truck0 depot1)
    (at truck1 depot1)
    (at truck2 distributor0)
    (at crate0 depot1)
    (at crate1 depot1)
    (at crate2 depot0)

    ;; Status of hoists
    (available hoist0)
    (available hoist1)
    (available hoist2)
    (available hoist3)

    ;; Stacking relations
    (on crate0 pallet1)
    (on crate1 crate0)
    (on crate2 pallet0)

    ;; Surface clarity
    (clear crate1)
    (clear crate2)
    (clear pallet2)
    (clear pallet3)
    ;; (pallet0, pallet1, and crate0 are not clear because they have crates on them)
  )

  (:goal
    (and
      (on crate1 pallet1)
      (on crate2 pallet3)
    )
  )
)
</problem>
================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain depots)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types
    place surface hoist truck - object
    depot distributor - place
    pallet crate - surface
  )

  (:predicates
    (at ?obj - object ?p - place)
    (on ?c - crate ?s - surface)
    (in ?c - crate ?t - truck)
    (lifting ?h - hoist ?c - crate)
    (available ?h - hoist)
    (clear ?s - surface)
  )

  (:action drive
    :parameters (?t - truck ?from - place ?to - place)
    :precondition (at ?t ?from)
    :effect (and
      (not (at ?t ?from))
      (at ?t ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action lift
    :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?s - surface ?p - place)
    :precondition (and
      (at ?h ?p)
      (at ?c ?p)
      (available ?h)
      (clear ?c)
      (on ?c ?s)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at ?c ?p))
      (lifting ?h ?c)
      (not (available ?h))
      (clear ?s)
      (not (on ?c ?s))
      (not (clear ?c))
    )
  )

  (:action drop
    :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?s - surface ?p - place)
    :precondition (and
      (at ?h ?p)
      (at ?s ?p)
      (clear ?s)
      (lifting ?h ?c)
    )
    :effect (and
      (available ?h)
      (not (lifting ?h ?c))
      (at ?c ?p)
      (not (clear ?s))
      (clear ?c)
      (on ?c ?s)
    )
  )

  (:action load
    :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?t - truck ?p - place)
    :precondition (and
      (at ?h ?p)
      (at ?t ?p)
      (lifting ?h ?c)
    )
    :effect (and
      (available ?h)
      (not (lifting ?h ?c))
      (in ?c ?t)
    )
  )

  (:action unload
    :parameters (?h - hoist ?c - crate ?t - truck ?p - place)
    :precondition (and
      (at ?h ?p)
      (at ?t ?p)
      (available ?h)
      (in ?c ?t)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (in ?c ?t))
      (not (available ?h))
      (lifting ?h ?c)
    )
  )
)

PROBLEM

(define (problem depots_problem)
  (:domain depots)
  (:objects
    depot0 depot1 depot2 - depot
    distributor0 - distributor
    truck0 truck1 truck2 - truck
    hoist0 hoist1 hoist2 hoist3 - hoist
    pallet0 pallet1 pallet2 pallet3 - pallet
    crate0 crate1 crate2 - crate
  )

  (:init
    ;; Locations of stationary objects
    (at hoist0 depot0)
    (at hoist1 depot1)
    (at hoist2 depot2)
    (at hoist3 distributor0)
    (at pallet0 depot0)
    (at pallet1 depot1)
    (at pallet2 depot2)
    (at pallet3 distributor0)

    ;; Locations of mobile objects
    (at truck0 depot1)
    (at truck1 depot1)
    (at truck2 distributor0)
    (at crate0 depot1)
    (at crate1 depot1)
    (at crate2 depot0)

    ;; Status of hoists
    (available hoist0)
    (available hoist1)
    (available hoist2)
    (available hoist3)

    ;; Stacking relations
    (on crate0 pallet1)
    (on crate1 crate0)
    (on crate2 pallet0)

    ;; Surface clarity
    (clear crate1)
    (clear crate2)
    (clear pallet2)
    (clear pallet3)
    ;; (pallet0, pallet1, and crate0 are not clear because they have crates on them)
  )

  (:goal
    (and
      (on crate1 pallet1)
      (on crate2 pallet3)
    )
  )
)

*Time: 00:10 - 00:15*

**Step 3: Load crate2**
Load **crate2** into **truck2** using **hoist0** at **depot0**.
*Time: 00:15 - 00:20*

**Step 4: Transport crate2**
Drive **truck2** from **depot0** to **distributor0**.
*Time: 00:20 - 00:30*

**Step 5: Unload crate2**
At **distributor0**, use **hoist3** to unload **crate2** from **truck2**.
*Time: 00:30 - 00:35*

**Step 6: Place crate2 (Goal reached for crate2)**

*Time: 00:35 - 00:40*

**Step 7: Clear crate0**
At **depot1**, use **hoist1** to lift **crate1** off of **crate0**.
*Time: 00:40 - 00:45*

**Step 8: Temporarily store crate1**
Load **crate1** into **truck1** at **depot1** using **hoist1**.
*Time: 00:45 - 00:50*

At **depot1**, use **hoist1** to lift **crate0** from **pallet1**.
*Time: 00:50 - 00:55*

**Step 10: Clear the area**
Load **crate0** into **truck1** at **depot1** using **hoist1**. (This clears pallet1 for the final goal).
*Time: 00:55 - 01:00*

**Step 11: Retrieve crate1**
Use **hoist1** to unload **crate1** from **truck1** at **depot1**.
*Time: 01:00 - 01:05*

**Step 12: Place crate1 (Goal reached for crate1)**
Drop **crate1** onto the now-clear **pallet1** at **depot1** using **hoist1**.
*Time: 01:05 - 01:10*

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot0, crate1 is at depot0, crate2 is at depot2, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at depot0, truck1 is at depot1, truck2 is at depot0, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate1 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet1 is clear, pallet3 is clear, crate0 is on pallet0, crate1 is on crate0 and crate2 is on pallet2.
My goal is to have that crate0 is on pallet3, crate1 is on pallet0 and crate2 is on crate0.

My plan is as follows:

Based on the provided PDDL plan and specification, here are the human-readable instructions to achieve the goal:

**Step 6: Load Crate 0 into Truck**
At **depot0**, use **hoist0** to load **crate0** into **truck0**.

**Step 7: Reposition Crate 1 for Unloading**
At **depot0**, use **hoist0** to unload **crate1** from **truck0** (holding it for placement).

**Step 8: Move Truck 0 to Depot 2**
Drive **truck0** from **depot0** to **depot2**.

**Step 9: Finalize Crate 1 Placement**
At **depot0**, use **hoist0** to drop **crate1** onto **pallet0**. (Goal for crate1 achieved).

**Step 10: Move Truck 0 to Distributor 0**
Drive **truck0** from **depot2** to **distributor0**.

**Step 11: Unload Crate 0 at Destination**
At **distributor0**, use **hoist3** to unload **crate0** from **truck0**.

**Step 12: Finalize Crate 0 Placement**
At **distributor0**, use **hoist3** to drop **crate0** onto **pallet3**. (Goal for crate0 achieved).

**Step 13: Return Truck 0 to Depot 2**
Drive **truck0** from **distributor0** to **depot2**.

Drive **truck0** from **depot2** to **distributor0**.

At **distributor0**, use **hoist3** to unload **crate2** from **truck0**.

**Step 17: Finalize Crate 2 Placement**
At **distributor0**, use **hoist3** to drop **crate2** onto the top of **crate0**. (Goal for crate2 achieved).

*   **Crate 1** is on **pallet 0**.
*   **Crate 2** is on **crate 0**.

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot2, crate1 is at depot0, crate2 is at depot2, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at depot2, truck1 is at depot0, truck2 is at depot2, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate1 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet1 is clear, pallet3 is clear, crate0 is on pallet2, crate1 is on pallet0 and crate2 is on crate0.
My goal is to have that crate1 is on pallet0 and crate2 is on pallet3.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot2, crate1 is at depot0, crate2 is at depot2, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at depot2, truck1 is at depot0, truck2 is at depot2, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate1 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet1 is clear, pallet3 is clear, crate0 is on pallet2, crate1 is on pallet0 and crate2 is on crate0.
My goal is to have that crate1 is on pallet0 and crate2 is on pallet3.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
Error while generating a response: Event loop is closed

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To achieve the goal of having **crate1 on pallet0** and **crate2 on pallet3**, follow these sequence of actions:

**Step 1: Lift crate2 at depot2**
- **Action:** Use **hoist2** to lift **crate2** from the top of **crate0** at **depot2**.
- **Timestamp:** 00:00 - 00:01
- **Status:** Crate2 is now being held by hoist2; crate0 is now clear.

**Step 2: Load crate2 into truck2**
- **Action:** Use **hoist2** to load **crate2** into **truck2**, which is also located at **depot2**.
- **Timestamp:** 00:01 - 00:02
- **Status:** Crate2 is now inside truck2; hoist2 is available for other tasks.

**Step 3: Transport crate2 to distributor0**
- **Action:** Drive **truck2** from **depot2** to **distributor0**.
- **Timestamp:** 00:02 - 00:03
- **Status:** Truck2 (carrying crate2) is now at distributor0.

**Step 4: Unload crate2 at distributor0**
- **Action:** Use **hoist3** (located at **distributor0**) to unload **crate2** from **truck2**.
- **Timestamp:** 00:03 - 00:04
- **Status:** Crate2 is now being held by hoist3; truck2 is empty.

**Step 5: Place crate2 onto pallet3**
- **Action:** Use **hoist3** to drop **crate2** onto **pallet3** at **distributor0**.
- **Timestamp:** 00:04 - 00:05
- **Status:** Crate2 is now on pallet3. 

**Goal finalized:** Crate1 remains on pallet0 (as per initial state), and crate2 is now successfully positioned on pallet3.
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot2, crate1 is at distributor0, crate2 is at depot1, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at depot1, truck1 is at depot1, truck2 is at depot1, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate0 is clear, crate1 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet0 is clear, crate0 is on pallet2, crate1 is on pallet3 and crate2 is on pallet1.
My goal is to have that crate0 is on pallet0, crate1 is on pallet1 and crate2 is on pallet3.

My plan is as follows:

A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot2, crate1 is at distributor0, crate2 is at depot1, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at depot1, truck1 is at depot1, truck2 is at depot1, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate0 is clear, crate1 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet0 is clear, crate0 is on pallet2, crate1 is on pallet3 and crate2 is on pallet1.
My goal is to have that crate0 is on pallet0, crate1 is on pallet1 and crate2 is on pallet3.

The plan is now complete, and all crates are in their designated goal positions.

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot0, crate1 is at depot0, crate2 is at depot1, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at depot2, truck1 is at depot1, truck2 is at depot2, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate1 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet2 is clear, pallet3 is clear, crate0 is on pallet0, crate1 is on crate0 and crate2 is on pallet1.
My goal is to have that crate0 is on pallet1, crate1 is on crate0 and crate2 is on pallet0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To fulfill the logistics requirements specified in the PDDL domain and problem, the following plan has been generated. This sequence of actions ensures that all crates are transported to their target locations and placed on the correct surfaces.

**Execution Plan:**

*   **Step 1 (00:00):** Drive **truck2** from **depot2** to **depot0**.
*   **Step 2 (00:05):** At **depot1**, use **hoist1** to lift **crate2** from **pallet1**.
*   **Step 3 (00:10):** At **depot1**, use **hoist1** to load **crate2** onto **truck1**.
*   **Step 4 (00:15):** At **depot0**, use **hoist0** to lift **crate1** from the top of **crate0**.
*   **Step 5 (00:20):** At **depot0**, use **hoist0** to load **crate1** onto **truck2**.
*   **Step 6 (00:25):** At **depot0**, use **hoist0** to lift **crate0** from **pallet0**.
*   **Step 7 (00:30):** At **depot0**, use **hoist0** to load **crate0** onto **truck2**.

*   **Step 9 (00:45):** At **depot1**, use **hoist1** to unload **crate0** from **truck2**.
*   **Step 10 (00:50):** Drive **truck1** (carrying crate2) from **depot1** to **depot0**.

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at distributor0, crate1 is at distributor0, crate2 is at distributor0, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at depot0, truck1 is at depot0, truck2 is at depot1, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate2 is clear, pallet0 is clear, pallet1 is clear, pallet2 is clear, crate0 is on pallet3, crate1 is on crate0 and crate2 is on crate1.
My goal is to have that crate1 is on pallet1 and crate2 is on pallet2.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================

Based on the PDDL domain, problem, and plan provided, here is the sequence of actions to transport the crates to their required destinations.

### Logistics Execution Plan:

**Step 1: Lift Crate 2 at Distributor 0**
(Timestamp: 0 to 1 minute)
Using **hoist3** at **distributor0**, lift **crate2** from the top of **crate1**. 
*Status: hoist3 is now lifting crate2; crate1 is clear.*

**Step 2: Relocate Truck 2**
(Timestamp: 1 to 2 minutes)
Drive **truck2** from **depot1** to **distributor0**.
*Status: truck2 is now available at distributor0 for loading.*

**Step 3: Load Crate 2 onto Truck 2**
(Timestamp: 2 to 3 minutes)
Using **hoist3**, load **crate2** into **truck2** at **distributor0**.
*Status: crate2 is inside truck2; hoist3 is now available.*

**Step 4: Lift Crate 1 at Distributor 0**
(Timestamp: 3 to 4 minutes)
Using **hoist3** at **distributor0**, lift **crate1** from the top of **crate0**.
*Status: hoist3 is now lifting crate1; crate0 is clear.*

**Step 5: Load Crate 1 onto Truck 2**
(Timestamp: 4 to 5 minutes)
Using **hoist3**, load **crate1** into **truck2** at **distributor0**.
*Status: Both crate1 and crate2 are inside truck2; hoist3 is now available.*

**Step 6: Move Truck 2 to Depot 1**
(Timestamp: 5 to 6 minutes)
Drive **truck2** from **distributor0** to **depot1**.
*Status: truck2 is now at depot1.*

**Step 7: Unload Crate 1 at Depot 1**
(Timestamp: 6 to 7 minutes)
Using **hoist1** at **depot1**, unload **crate1** from **truck2**.
*Status: hoist1 is lifting crate1; truck2 still contains crate2.*

**Step 8: Move Truck 2 to Depot 2**
(Timestamp: 7 to 8 minutes)
Drive **truck2** from **depot1** to **depot2**.
*Status: truck2 is now at depot2.*

**Step 9: Unload Crate 2 at Depot 2**
(Timestamp: 8 to 9 minutes)
Using **hoist2** at **depot2**, unload **crate2** from **truck2**.
*Status: hoist2 is lifting crate2; truck2 is now empty.*

**Step 10: Place Crate 1 on Pallet 1**
(Timestamp: 9 to 10 minutes)
Using **hoist1**, drop **crate1** onto **pallet1** at **depot1**.
*Status: Goal partially met (crate1 on pallet1).*

**Step 11: Place Crate 2 on Pallet 2**
(Timestamp: 10 to 11 minutes)
Using **hoist2**, drop **crate2** onto **pallet2** at **depot2**.
*Status: Goal fully met (crate1 on pallet1 and crate2 on pallet2).*

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot2, crate1 is at depot0, crate2 is at depot0, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at distributor0, truck1 is at distributor0, truck2 is at depot2, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate0 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet1 is clear, pallet3 is clear, crate0 is on pallet2, crate1 is on pallet0 and crate2 is on crate1.
My goal is to have that crate0 is on crate2, crate1 is on pallet2 and crate2 is on pallet1.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]

At **depot0**, use **hoist0** to lift **crate1** from **pallet0**.

**Step 6: 00:25 - 00:30**
At **depot0**, use **hoist0** to load **crate1** into **truck2**.

**Step 7: 00:30 - 00:35**
Drive **truck2** (carrying **crate1** and **crate2**) from **depot0** to **depot1**.

**Step 8: 00:35 - 00:40**
At **depot1**, use **hoist1** to unload **crate2** from **truck2**.

**Step 9: 00:40 - 00:45**
At **depot1**, use **hoist1** to drop **crate2** onto **pallet1**.

**Step 10: 00:45 - 00:50**
Drive **truck2** (carrying **crate1**) from **depot1** to **depot2**.

**Step 11: 00:50 - 00:55**
At **depot2**, use **hoist2** to load **crate0** (which it has been holding since Step 2) into **truck2**.

**Step 12: 00:55 - 01:00**
At **depot2**, use **hoist2** to unload **crate1** from **truck2**.

**Step 13: 01:00 - 01:05**
Drive **truck2** (carrying **crate0**) from **depot2** to **depot1**.

At **depot1**, use **hoist1** to unload **crate0** from **truck2**.

**Step 15: 01:10 - 01:15**
At **depot2**, use **hoist2** to drop **crate1** onto **pallet2**.

**Step 16: 01:15 - 01:20**
At **depot1**, use **hoist1** to drop **crate0** onto **crate2**.

- **crate0** is stacked on **crate2** at **depot1**.
- **crate1** is on **pallet2** at **depot2**.

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot1, crate1 is at distributor0, crate2 is at depot0, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at depot0, truck1 is at distributor0, truck2 is at depot1, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate0 is clear, crate1 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet2 is clear, crate0 is on pallet1, crate1 is on pallet3 and crate2 is on pallet0.
My goal is to have that crate0 is on pallet3, crate1 is on crate2 and crate2 is on pallet1.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot1, crate1 is at distributor0, crate2 is at depot1, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at depot1, truck1 is at depot2, truck2 is at distributor0, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate1 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet0 is clear, pallet2 is clear, crate0 is on pallet1, crate1 is on pallet3 and crate2 is on crate0.
My goal is to have that crate0 is on pallet0 and crate2 is on pallet1.

My plan is as follows:

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan the logistics of transporting crates between a number of depots and distributors via trucks that are loaded by hoists. Depots and distributors are directly connected by roads (trucks can drive between any two depots or distributors).

A depot is a type of place.
A distributor is a type of place.
A pallet is a type of surface.
A crate is a type of surface.

Here are the actions that can be performed:

Drive a truck from one place to another place.
Use a hoist to lift a crate from a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to drop a crate to a surface at a place.
Use a hoist to load a crate into a truck at a place.
Use a hoist to unload a crate from a truck at a place.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A truck can be driven from one place to another place only if the truck is at the origin place.
Once a truck has been driven from one place to another, it is not at the origin place and is at the destination place.
A crate can be lifted by a hoist only if the hoist is at the same place as the crate, the hoist is available, and the crate is clear.
Once a crate has been lifted by a hoist from a surface at a place, the crate is not at the place, the hoist is lifting the crate, the hoist is not available, the surface is clear, and the crate is not on the surface.
A crate can be dropped by a hoist to a surface only if the hoist and surface are both at the place, the surface is clear, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been dropped by a hoist to a surface at a place, the hoist is available, the hoist is not lifting the crate, the crate is at the place, the surface is not clear, the crate is clear, and the crate is on the surface.
A crate can be loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place, the truck is at the same place, and the hoist is lifting the crate.
Once a crate has been loaded by a hoist onto a truck at a place, 
A crate can be unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place only if the hoist is at the same place as the truck, the hoist is available, and the crate is in the truck.
Once a crate has been unloaded by a hoist from a truck at a place, the crate is not in the truck, the hoist is not available, and the hoist is lifting the crate.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, crate0 is at depot1, crate1 is at depot2, crate2 is at distributor0, hoist0 is at depot0, hoist1 is at depot1, hoist2 is at depot2, hoist3 is at distributor0, pallet0 is at depot0, pallet1 is at depot1, pallet2 is at depot2, pallet3 is at distributor0, truck0 is at distributor0, truck1 is at depot2, truck2 is at depot0, hoist0 is available, hoist1 is available, hoist2 is available, hoist3 is available, crate0 is clear, crate1 is clear, crate2 is clear, pallet0 is clear, crate0 is on pallet1, crate1 is on pallet2 and crate2 is on pallet3.
My goal is to have that crate0 is on pallet1, crate1 is on pallet2 and crate2 is on pallet0.
